(vi-rai) piṭiyaik keṭuttamaiyāl, kōṭṭilē kaiyai vaitta yāṉai yeṉka. maṟutta uyakkam - peyareccam, kāraṇap poruṭṭu. paṇpu - īṇṭu aḻakiṉ mēṟṟu; ilakkaṇattāl tiruntutal eṉṟumām. aṟīi - aṟintu vaittum eṉka. tātu - poṉ. tītu iṉṟāka - yāṉ piritalākiya tīṅku nikaḻāmaikku. pukukeṉa - pukukeṉak kūṟi eṉa viritturaikka. eṉṉum - ciṟitum. taṇṭutal - nīṅkutal. ollutal - poruntutal. cēṇōṉ - uyarnta iṭattil iruppavaṉ - kuṟavaṉ. veṭi - ōcai. yāṉai irintu ōṭa eṟiyum kavaṇ kalliṉ ōcaiyālum, puliyiṉ muḻakkattālum, yāṉaiyiṉ kataṟutalālum malai muḻutum añcum eṉka. avaṟṟai mēkattiṉ muḻakka meṉakkoṇṭu mayil ālumeṉka. veṟpaṉ pukukeṉak kūṟi nīṅkumāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam nīṅkutal naṉi iṉṉātu, āyiṉ avaṉ avvāṟu pirital aritu. aḵtaṉṟi orōvaḻip piriyunaṉ āyiṉ, yāṉ avaṉai niṉpāṟ cērttu niṉatu nalattai mīṭṭut taruvēṉ eṉṟu tōḻi talaimakaṭkuk kuṟai nayappak kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. viṉaitavap peyarnta eṉṟatu, muṉ pōr ceytu āṟṟātu ōṭiya eṉṟapaṭi. viḻai taka ōṅkiya kaḻai tuñcu maruṅkiṟ kāṉamar naṉṉaṉ eṉṟataṉāl, kāṭci viruppattāl, naṉṉaṉ oru kāṭṭil vatintāṉātal vēṇṭum. tol pukaḻ peṟṟa naṉṉaṉ eṉavum kāṉamar naṉṉaṉ eṉavum kūṭṭuka. naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya araṇiṉai māṟṟu vēntaṉ oruvaṉ muṟṟip porutu tōṟṟu ōṭiyavaṉ, piṉ periya tāṉaiyuṭaṉ mīṭṭum vantu taṅka, araṇiluḷḷār avaṉuṭaṉ porutaṟku āṟṟātirukka, kāṭṭi
lirunta naṉṉaṉ mīḷa vantu taṉatu paḻaiya pukaḻ nilaipeṟumpaṭi ap pakaivaṉai veṉṟu araṇil uḷḷāraip pātukāttaṉaṉ. ākaliṉ an naṉṉaṉpōla niṉ nalam taruvēṉ eṉṟāḷ eṉka.
393. pālai
(piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		kōṭuyar piṟaṅkaṟ kuṉṟupala nīnti
		vēṟupulam paṭarnta viṉaitaral uḷḷattu
		āṟucel vampalar 1kāypaci tīriya
		2itaiccuvaṟ kalitta īrilai neṭuntōṭṭuk
	5.	kavaikkatir varakiṉ kāltoku poṅkaḻi
		kavaṭṭaṭip poruta 3palciṉai utirvai
		akaṉkaṭ 4pāṟaic cevvayiṟ ṟeṟīi
		variyaṇi paṇaittōḷ 5vārcevit taṉṉaiyar
		paṇṇai veṇpaḻat tarici ēyppac
	10.	cuḻalmarañ colitta cuḷakalai veṇkāḻ
		toṭimāṇ ulakkai ūḻiṟ pōkki
		uralmukaṅ kāṭṭiya curainiṟai koḷḷai
		āṅkaṇ iruñcuṉai nīroṭu mukavāk
		kaḷipaṭu kuḻicik kallaṭup pēṟṟi
	15.	iṇartatai kaṭukkai īṇṭiya tātiṟ
		kuṭavar puḻukkiya poṅkaviḻp puṉkam
		matarvai nallāṉ pāloṭu pakukkum
		niraipala kuḻīiya neṭumoḻip pulli
		tēṉṟūṅku uyarvarai naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
	20.	vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum āṇṭavar
		nīṭalar vāḻi tōḻi tōṭukoḷ
		urukeḻu maññai olicīr ēyppat
		takaram maṇṇiya taṇṇaṟu muccip
		pukaril kuvaḷaip pōtoṭu 1teriyitaḻ
	25.	vēṉil atiral vēyntaniṉ
		ēmuṟu puṇarcci iṉtuyil maṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 21. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, (nam talaivar,)
1-2. kōṭu uyar piṟaṅkal kuṉṟu pala nīnti - cikaram uyarnta pāṟaikaḷaiyuṭaiya malaikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṭantu, vēṟu pulam paṭarnta viṉaitaral uḷḷattu - vēṟṟu nāṭṭai aṭainta poruḷ tēṭum muyaṟciyaiyuṭaiya uḷḷattāl,
3-20. āṟu cel vampalar - vaḻic cellum putiyaratu, kāy paci tīriya - mikka paci oḻiya, itai cuval kalitta - putuk kollaiyil taḻaitta, īr ilai neṭu tōṭṭu kavai katirvarakiṉ kāl toku poṅkaḻi - īriya ilaiyiṉ nīṇṭa itaḻaiyuṭaiya varakiṉ kavartta katiruṭaṉ kūṭiya taṭṭaikaḷait tokutta poliyil, kavaṭṭu aṭi poruta palciṉai utirvai - māṭukaḷiṉ kavartta kuḷampāl tuvaikkap paṭṭa pala kiḷaikaḷiṉiṉṟu utirnta varakiṉai, akaṉ kaṇ pāṟai cevvayiṉ teṟīi - iṭam akaṉṟa pāṟaiyil cevviya iṭattil kuvittu, vari aṇi paṇai tōḷ vār cevi taṉṉaiyar - varikaḷ poruntiya parutta tōḷkaḷaiyum nīṇṭa cevikaḷaiyu muṭaiya tāyar, paṇṇai veṇ paḻattu arici ēyppa - paṇṇaik kīraiyiṉ veḷḷiya paḻattiṉ ariciyai oppa, cuḻal maram colitta cuḷaku alai veṇ kāḻ - tirimaram tēytta cuḷakiṉāl koḻikkappeṟṟa veḷḷiya ariciyai, toṭi māṇ ulakkai ūḻil pōkki - pūṇ māṭcimaippaṭṭa ulakkaiyāl muṟaiyākac celutti, ural mukam kāṭṭiya - uraliṟ peytu tīṭṭiya, curai niṟai koḷḷai - uraliṉ kuḻi niṟainta avvariciyai, āṅkaṇ iru cuṉai nīroṭu mukavā - aṅkuḷḷa periya cuṉaiyiṉ nīroṭu mukantu, kaḷi paṭu kuḻici kal aṭuppu ēṟṟi - maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāṉaiyil kaṟkaḷai aṭukki yamaitta aṭuppil ēṟṟi, iṇar tatai kaṭukkai īṇṭiya tātil - kottukkaḷ niṟainta koṉṟaiyiṉ niṟainta pūn tātu pōla, kuṭavar puḻukkiya poṅku aviḻ puṉkam- iṭaiyar ākkiya viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aviḻākiya cōṟṟai, matarvai nal āṉ pāloṭu pakukkum - matartta nalla pacuviṉ pāluṭaṉ kūṭṭi aḷikkum iṭamākiya, nirai pala kuḻīiya - pacu niraikaḷai mikap peṟṟa, neṭu moḻi pulli - mikka pukaḻuṭaiya pulli eṉpāṉatu, tēṉ tūṅku uyar varai nal nāṭṭu umpar - tēṉiṟāl toṅkum uyarnta malaikaḷaiyuṭaiya nalla nāṭukaṭku appāṟ
paṭṭa, vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - vēṅkaṭa malaiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟuḷḷā rāyiṉum,
21-26. tōṭukoḷ urukeḻu maññai oli cīr ēyppa - aḻaku poruntiya mayiliṉ tokuti koṇṭa taḻaitta tōkaiyiṉai oppa, takaram maṇṇiya taṇ naṟu mucci - mayirc cāntu pūcap peṟṟa taṇṇiya naṟiya kūntalil, pukar il kuvaḷaip pōtoṭu teri itaḻ vēṉil atiral vēynta - kuṟṟamillāta kuvaḷaip pūvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya vēṉiliṟ pūkkum kāṭṭu mallikaip pūviṉaiyum cūṭiya, niṉ ēm uṟu puṇarcci iṉ tuyil maṟantu - niṉatu iṉpam vāynta kūṭṭamākiya iṉiya tuyiliṉai maṟantu,
20-21. āṇṭu avar nīṭalar - āṅkaṇ avar tāḻttiruppār allar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! nam talaivar, kuṉṟu pala nīnti, viṉai taral uḷḷattāl, vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ ēmuṟu puṇarcci iṉtuyil maṟantu, āṇṭu avar nīṭalar.
(vi-rai) paṭarnta viṉaitaral uḷḷam - paṭarntu poruḷ tarutalaiyuṭaiya āḷviṉaiyuṭaiya uḷḷam eṉka. uḷḷattāl vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum eṉak kūṭṭuka. uḷḷattaiyuṭaiya āṟu cel vampalar eṉṟalumām. itaic cuval - kāṭu tiruttiya mēṭṭu nilamākiya putuk kollai. varakiṉ katirkaḷait taṭṭaikaḷoṭu koytu kuvittu vaipparākaliṉ, kavaik katir varakiṉ kāl toku poṅkaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. kāl - taṭṭai. poṅkaḻi - tūṟṟāp poli. kavaṭṭu aṭi - māṭukaḷiṉ kālkaḷiluḷḷa kavartta kuḷampukaḷ. utirvai - utirnta tāṉiya maṇi. teṟīi - kuvittu vaittuk koṇṭu, paṇṇai - oruvakaik kīrai; ataṉ paḻam veḷḷoḷi poruntiya katirāka irukkum; ataṉ ariciyai yoppac colitta veṇkāḻ eṉka. cuḻal maram - tiri maram, tirikai. colittal - tēyttal. ulakkai ūḻiṟ pōkki ural mukam kāṭṭiya eṉṟatu, uraliṟ peytu ciṟitu tīṭṭiya eṉṟapaṭi. curai - uraliṉ kuḻi; kuḻiciyiṉ uḷḷiṭam eṉṟalumām. kuṭavar - āyar. vampalar paci tīriya taṉṉaiyar puṉkam pāloṭu pakukkum eṉka. pāloṭu pakukkum naṉṉāṭu eṉavum, nirai pala kuḻīiya naṉṉāṭu eṉavum, pulliyiṉ naṉṉāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. neṭumoḻi - vañciṉamumām. naṉṉāṭṭumpar pulliyiṉ vēṅkaṭam eṉak kūṭṭutalumām. oli cīr ēyppa - taḻaitta tōkaiyiṉ aḻakiṉai yoppa eṉka.
394. mullai
(iravukkuṟit talaimakaḷai iṭattuyttu vantu tōḻi 
talaimakaṉai varaivu kaṭāayatu.)
		kaḷavum puḷittaṉa viḷavum paḻuniṉa
		ciṟutalait turuviṉ paḻuppuṟu viḷaitayir
		itaippuṉa varakiṉ avaippumāṇ ariciyoṭu
		kārvāyt toḻinta īrvāyp puṟṟattu
	5.	īyalpey taṭṭa iṉpuḷi veñcōṟu
		cētāṉ veṇṇey vempuṟat turuka
		iḷaiyar aruntap piṉṟai nīyum
		iṭumuḷ vēli muṭakkāṟ pantarp
		putukkalat taṉṉa cevvāyc ciṟṟil
	10.	puṉaiyiruṅ katuppiṉiṉ maṉaiyō ḷayarap
		pāluṭai aṭicil toṭīiya orunāḷ
		māvaṇ tōṉṟal vantaṉai ceṉmō
		kāṭuṟai iṭaiyaṉ yāṭutalaip peyarkkum
		maṭiviṭu vīḷai verīik kuṟumuyal
	15.	maṉṟa irumputal oḷikkum
		puṉpula vaippiṉeñ ciṟunal lūrē.
- naṉpalūrc ciṟumētāviyār.
(co-ḷ) 12. māvaṇ tōṉṟal - mikka vaṇmaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!
13-16. kāṭu uṟai iṭaiyaṉ - kāṭṭil taṅkum iṭaiyaṉ, yāṭutalai peyarkkum - taṉ yāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷai oruvaḻik kūṭṭum, maṭi viṭu vīḷai - vāyai maṭittu eḻuppum cīḻkkai yoliyai, verīi - kēṭṭu añci, kuṟumuyal - kuṟumuyalāṉatu, maṉṟa irumputal oḷikkum - maṉṟattiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa periya putaril maṟaintu koḷḷum, puṉpula vaippiṉ - mullai nilattu ūrākiya, emciṟu nal ūr ematu ciṟiya nalla ūriṉkaṇ,
1. kaḷavum puḷittaṉa - kaḷāvum paḻuttup puḷippuc cuvaiyai eytiṉa, viḷavum paḻuniṉa - viḷāvum mutirntu paḻuttaṉa,
2-11. ciṟu talai turuviṉ paḻuppu uṟu viḷai tayir - ciṟiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya cemmaṟiyāṭṭiṉatu paḻuppu niṟam vāynta muṟṟiya tayirākiya ulaiyil, itai puṉa varakiṉ avaippu māṇ ariciyoṭu - putuk kollaiyil viḷainta varakiṉatu kuttutal māṭcimaippaṭṭa ariciyuṭaṉ, kār vāyttu īr vāyp puṟṟattu oḻinta - kārmaḻai mikkamaiyāl naṉainta vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya puṟṟiliruntu veḷippaṭ ṭoḻinta, īyal peytu aṭṭa - īyalaiyum iṭṭuc camaitta, iṉ puḷi vem cōṟu - iṉiya puḷippu vāynta viruppam tarum cōṟṟiṉai, cētāṉ veṇṇey vem puṟattu uruka - civanta pacuviṉ veṇṇey veppam poruntiya ac cōṟṟiṉ mēlē kiṭantu uruka ataṉ mēṟ peytu, iḷaiyar arunta - niṉ ēvalar muṉpu uṇṇa, piṉṟai - ataṉ piṉpu, nīyum, iṭumuḷ vēli muṭa kāl pantar - muḷḷiṭṭuk kaṭṭiya vēliyiṉakattē vaḷainta kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya pantaraiyuṭaiyatum, putukkalattu aṉṉa cevvāy ciṟṟil - putukkalam pōṉṟa cemmaṇ pūciyatumākiya  ciṟiya illil, puṉai irum katuppiṉ niṉ maṉaiyōḷ ayara - puṉainta kariya kūntalaiyuṭaiya niṉ maṉaivi viruntayara, pāl uṭai aṭicil toṭīiya - pāl peyta uṇaviṉai uṇṇutaṟku, oru nāḷ-,
12. vantaṉai ceṉmō - vantu celvāyāka,
(muṭipu) māvaṇ tōṉṟal! em ciṟu nallūr, kaḷavum puḷittaṉa, viḷavum paḻuniṉa. iṉpuḷi veñcōṟu veṇṇey puṟatturuka iḷaiyar arunta, piṉpu nīyum cevvāyc ciṟṟil, maṉaiyōḷ ayara pāluṭai aṭicil toṭīiya oru nāḷ vantaṉai ceṉmō.
(vi-rai) puḷittaṉa - puḷiccuvai eytap paḻuttaṉa eṉṟapaṭi. kaḷāp paḻaṅkaḷ paḻuttaṉa, viḷām paḻaṅkaḷ mutirntaṉa eṉṟataṉ karuttu, viruntayartaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa yāvum uḷḷaṉa eṉpatākum. veṇṇey urukap peytu eṉa oru col varuvikka. cevvāyc ciṟṟil eṉṟataṉālum, puṉaiyiruṅ katuppiṉ niṉ maṉaiyōḷ ayarap pāluṭai aṭicil eṉṟataṉālum talaiviyai nī varaintu koṇṭu viruntuṇṭaṟku varuka eṉak kuṟittavāṟāyiṟṟu.
395. pālai
(piriviṭait tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		taṇkayam payanta vaṇkāṟ kuvaḷai
		māri māmalar peyaṟkēṟ ṟaṉṉa
		nīroṭu niṟainta pēramar maḻaikkaṇ
		paṉivār evvam tīra iṉivariṉ
	5.	naṉṟumaṉ vāḻi tōḻi teṟukatir
		īram naitta nīraṟu naṉantalai
		aḻaṉmēyn tuṇṭa niḻaṉmāy iyaviṉ
		vaṟaṉmarat taṉṉa kavaimarup peḻiṟkalai
		aṟalavirn taṉṉa tērnacai yōṭip
	10.	pulampuvaḻip paṭṭa ulamaral 1uḷḷamoṭu
		mēypiṇaip payirum melintaḻi paṭarkural
		aruñcurañ cellunar āṭcet tōrkkun
		tiruntarai ñemaiya perumpuṉak kuṉṟattu
		āṭukaḻai iruvetir naraluṅ
	15.	kōṭukāy kaṭaṟṟa kāṭiṟan tōrē.
- eyiṉantai makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
5-15. teṟu katir īram naitta nīr aṟu naṉantalai - oṟukkum ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir īrattiṉait tolaittamaiyāṉ nīr aṟṟa akaṉṟa iṭattiluḷḷa, aḻal mēyntu uṇṭa niḻal māy iyaviṉ - tī kavarntu uṇṭamaiyāl maraṅkaḷiṉ niḻalum oḻinta neṟiyil, vaṟal marattaṉṉa kavai maruppu eḻil kalai - vaṟṟiya marak kompiṉai yotta kavartta kompiṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya kalaimāṉ, aṟal avirntaṉṉa tēr nacai ōṭi -aṟal viḷaṅkiṉā lotta pēyt tēriṉai nīreṉa virumpi yōṭi, pulampu vaḻippaṭṭa ulamaral uḷḷamoṭu - taṉimaiyuṟṟamaiyālāṉa kalaṅkiya uḷḷattuṭaṉ, mēy piṇai payirum -mēyum taṉ peṇ māṉai aḻaikkum, melintu aḻi paṭar kural - melivuṟṟu oḻiyum tuṉpiṉait tarum kuralai, arum curam cellunar āḷ cettu ōrkkum - ariya kāṭṭiṉiṟ celluvōr āḷiṉ kuraleṉṟu karuti ārāyum iṭamākiya, tiruntu arai ñemaiya perum puṉak kuṉṟattu - cevviya aṭi poruntiya ñemai maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya periya kollaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kuṉṟil, āṭu kaḻai iru vetir naralum - acaiyum taṇṭiṉaiyuṭaiya periya mūṅkilkaḷ olikkum, kōṭu kāy kaṭaṟṟa kāṭu iṟantōr - cimaiyam vempiya malaip pakkattiluḷḷavākiya kāṭukaḷaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talaivar,
1-5. taṇkayam payanta vaṇ kāl kuvaḷai - kuḷirnta kuḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟiya vaḷaviya kāmpiṉaiyuṭaiya kuvaḷaiyiṉ, māri mā malar - maḻaikkālattē malarnta kariya malar, peyaṟku ēṟṟaṉṉa - maḻaikku etirppaṭṭā lotta, nīroṭu niṟainta pēr amar maḻai kaṇ - nīrāl niṟainta periya amartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟu, paṉi vār evvam tīra - nīr oḻukum tuṉpam oḻiya, iṉivariṉ naṉṟu - ippoḻutu varuvārāyiṉ naṉṟākum, maṉ - vantilarē, eṉ ceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi!vāḻi! kāṭiṟantōr, maḻaikkaṇ evvam tīra iṉivariṉ naṉṟu; maṉ.
(vi-rai) naitta - aḻitta `vēlē .... nāṭu naittaliṉ'1 eṉpuḻi naittal ip poruṭṭātal kāṇka. aḻal mēyntu uṇṭa - `uṇṭaṟ kuriya vallāp poruḷai, uṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalu marapē'2  eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. aṟal - aṟalpaṭa oḻukum ciṟiya nīr. nīraṟu naṉantalai iyaviṉ kalai ōṭi uḷḷamoṭu piṇaip payir kural eṉka. kuralaic curam cellunar ōrkkum kāṭu eṉavum, kuṉṟattu vetir naralum kaṭaṟṟa kāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
396. marutam
(kātaṟ parattai talaimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		toṭutteṉ makiḻna cellal koṭittērp
		polampūṇ naṉṉaṉ 3 puṉaṉāṭu kaṭinteṉa
		yāḻicai maṟukiṟ pāḻi āṅkaṇ
		añcal eṉṟa āay eyiṉaṉ
	5.	ikalaṭu kaṟpiṉ miñiliyoṭu tākkit
		taṉṉuyir 4koṭuttaṉaṉ colliya tamaiyātu
		teṟalaruṅ kaṭavuḷ muṉṉart 5tēṟṟi
		melliṟai muṉkai paṟṟiya colliṟantu
		ārva neñcan talaittalai ciṟappaniṉ
	10.	mārputaru kallāy piṟaṉā yiṉaiyē
		iṉiyāṉ viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ manti
		paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ palapulan tuṟaiyak
		kaṭuntiṟal atti āṭaṇi nacaii
		neṭunīrk kāviri koṇṭoḷit tāṅkuniṉ
	15.	maṉaiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval ciṉaii
		āriyar alaṟat tākkip pēricait
		toṉṟumutir vaṭavarai vaṇaṅkuviṟ poṟittu
		veñciṉa vēntaraip 1piṇittōṉ
		vañci aṉṉaveṉ nalantantu ceṉmē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1. makiḻna - talaivaṉē! toṭutteṉ - niṉṉai yāṉ paṟṟik koṇṭēṉ, cellal - cellātē;
1-6. koṭi tēr polam pūṇ naṉṉaṉ - koṭiyaṇinta tēriṉaiyum poṉṉālāya pūṇkaḷaiyumuṭaiya naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, puṉṉāṭu kaṭinteṉa - puṉṉāṭeṉṉum nāṭṭi ṉuḷḷārai vekuṇṭeḻuntāṉāka, yāḻ icai maṟukiṉ pāḻi āṅkaṇ - yāḻiṉ icai poruntiya terukkaḷaiyuṭaiya pāḻi eṉṉum nakariṭattē niṉṟu, añcal eṉṟa - añcātīr eṉṟu kūṟiya, āay eyiṉaṉ - āay eyiṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, colliyatu amaiyātu - tāṉ colliya coṟ piḻaiyātu, ikal aṭu kaṟpiṉ miñiliyoṭu tākki - pōr vellum payiṟciyaiyuṭaiya miñili eṉpāṉoṭu porutu, taṉ uyir koṭuttaṉaṉ - taṉ uyiraiyum tantāṉ; (nīyō), 
7-11. teṟal aru kaṭavuḷ muṉṉar tēṟṟi - teṟutaṟku ariya teyvattiṉ muṉ teḷivittu, mel iṟai muṉ kai paṟṟiya col iṟantu - melliya cantiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉ muṉ kaiyiṉaip paṟṟic colliya colliṉaik kaṭantu, ārva neñcam talai talai ciṟappa - aṉpu mikka uḷḷa mēṉmēlum makiḻntu ciṟakka, niṉ mārpu tarukallāy - niṉ mārpiṉait tārāyāy, piṟaṉ āyiṉaiyē- ētilaṉ āyiṉaiyē, iṉi yāṉ viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ - iṉi yāṉ niṉṉai viṭuppēṉ allēṉ;
11-15. manti paṉi vār kaṇṇaḷ pala pulantu uṟaiya - ātimanti eṉpāḷ nīr oḻukum kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiyaḷāyp palavaṟṟaiyum veṟuttirukka, kaṭu tiṟal atti āṭu aṇi nacaii - kaṭiya tiṟam poruntiya āṭṭaṉ atti eṉṉum avaḷ kātalaṉ āṭum aḻakiṉai virumpi, neṭu nīrk kāviri koṇṭoḷittāṅku - nīrp perukkiṉaiyuṭaiya kāviriyāṟu koṇṭu maṟaintāṟpōla, niṉ maṉaiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval - niṉ maṉaiyāḷ niṉṉaip paṟṟikkōṭalum añcāniṟpēṉ;
15-19. ciṉaii - vekuṇṭeḻuntu, āriyar alaṟat tākki - āriya maṉṉarkaḷ alaṟumāṟu avarkaḷait tākki, pēr icai toṉṟu mutir vaṭavarai vaṇaṅku vil poṟittu - periya pukaḻaiyuṭaiya paḻaiyatākiya mutirnta imaiya malaiyiṉmītu vaḷainta viṟpoṟiyaip patittu, vem ciṉa vēntaraip piṇittōṉ - koṭiya ciṉam poruntiya pakai vēntaraip piṇittu vantōṉākiya cēraṉatu, vañci yaṉṉa - vañcinakarai yotta, eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē - eṉ aḻakiṉait tantu celvāyāka.
(muṭipu) makiḻna! toṭutteṉ; cellal. āay eyiṉaṉ colliyatu amaiyātu, taṉ uyir koṭuttaṉaṉ; (nīyō) kaṭavuḷ muṉṉart tēṟṟi muṉkai paṟṟiya col iṟantu mārpu tarukallāy piṟaṉ āyiṉai. iṉi yāṉ viṭukkuveṉ alleṉ. manti pala pulantu uṟaiya, atti āṭaṇi nacaii, kāviri koṇ ṭoḻittāṅku, niṉ maṉaiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval; vañci yaṉṉa eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē.
(vi-rai) toṭutteṉ - vaḷaittēṉ, paṟṟiṉēṉ. puṉṉāṭu -kuṭa kaṭaṟ pakkattatoru nāṭu, puḷḷunāṭu eṉpatum pāṭamākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, puṇṇāṭeṉpatu puṉṉāṭeṉac citaintatu eṉalumām. yāḻicai maṟuku eṉṟamaiyāl, pāḻiyiṉkaṇ paricil peṟum pāṇar mutalāyiṉār iṭaiyaṟātu varuvar eṉpatu peṟṟām. naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉukku āay eyiṉaṉ pakaivaṉum, miñili naṭpāḷaṉum āvar eṉpatum, āay eyiṉaṉ pāḻiyiṉkaṇ miñiliyoṭu porutu uyir tuṟantaṉaṉ eṉpatum, muṉṉar `yāma iraviṉ'1 eṉṉum ceyyuḷuṭ pōntamai kāṇka. colliyatu amaiyātu - tāṉ colliya coṟ piḻaiyātu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. tēṟṟi - teḷivittu. tēṟṟi muṉ kai paṟṟiya col eṉṟataṉai, muṉ kai paṟṟit tēṟṟiya col eṉa māṟuka. kaṭavuḷ muṉṉarc cūḷuraittut tēṟṟuvatu, `muṉṟēṟṟu' eṉappaṭum eṉpatu, `kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu muṉṟēṟṟu eṉa varuvataṉālum, ataṟku uraiyāciriyar palarum `kaṭuñcūḷ taruku veṉ niṉakkē'3 eṉpataṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭi yiruttalālum peṟappaṭum. eṉavē akanāṉūṟṟup patippukkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tōṟṟi eṉṉum pāṭattiṉum tēṟṟi* yeṉpatē ciṟappuṭait tātal kāṇka. āay eyiṉaṉ, oru kāl colliya coṟ piḻaiyātu, ētilār poruṭṭut taṉ uyiraiyum koṭuttaṉaṉ; nīyō niṉakku iṉiyaḷākak koṇṭu eṉatu nalattaiyum nukarntu, piṉ kaṭavuḷ muṉṉarc cūḷuṭaṉ kūṟiya uraiyaiyum tappi eṉṉaik kai viṭuttup piṟaṉ āyiṉai eṉat talaivaṉ koṭumaiyai avaṉ uṇarumāṟu kātaṟ parattai eṭutturaittu, attakaiya niṉṉai viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ eṉṟāḷ eṉka. manti - ātimanti, atti - āṭṭaṉatti. āriyar alaṟat tākki vaṭa varaiyil viṟ poṟittavaṉ, imaya varampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉṉum cēra maṉṉaṉ; itaṉai, `imaiyam viṟ poṟittu' eṉavum `pēricai marapiṉ āriyar vaṇakki' eṉavum patiṟṟuppattiṉ iraṇṭām pattup patikattil neṭuñcēralātaṉ kūṟappaṭutalāṉ aṟika. in nikaḻccikaḷ avaṉ maintaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉālum nikaḻntamai cilappatikārattāl aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, piṇittōṉ eṉṟatu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉalum poruntum.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷaik kāṭṭi, itu kāmakkiḻatti eṉ nalam tā eṉṟatu eṉṟaṉar nac.
397. pālai
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		eṉmakaḷ perumaṭam yāṉpā rāṭṭat
		tāytaṉ cemmal kaṇṭukaṭaṉ iṟuppa
		muḻavumukam pularā viḻavuṭai viyaṉakar
		maṇaṉiṭai yākak koḷḷāṉ kalpakak
	5.	kaṇamaḻai tuṟanta kāṉmayaṅ kaḻuvam
		eḷiya vāka ēntukoṭi paranta
		poṟivari alkul māa yōṭkeṉat
		taṇinta paruvañ cellāṉ paṭartarat
		tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa tuṉaiveṅ kāḷai
	10.	kaṭumpakaṭ ṭoruttal naṭuṅkak kuttip
		pōḻpuṇ paṭutta poriyarai ōmaip
		perumpoḷic cēyarai nōkki ūṉcettuk
		karuṅkāl yāttup paruntuvan tiṟukkum
		cēṇuyarn tōṅkiya vāṉuyar neṭuṅkōṭṭuk
	15.	kōṭai vevvaḷik kulamarum
		pullilai vetira nelviḷai kāṭē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tuṉai vem kāḷai - viraitalaiyuṭaiya vevviya kāḷai pōṉṟavaṉ,
1-4. muḻavu mukam pularā viḻavu uṭai viyaṉ nakar - muḻaviṉ oli māṟāta viḻāvuṭaiya akaṉṟa maṉaiyil, eṉ makaḷ peru maṭam yāṉ pārāṭṭa - eṉ makaḷatu periya maṭappattiṉai yāṉ pārāṭṭavum, tāy - naṟṟāy, taṉ cemmal kaṇṭu kaṭaṉ iṟuppa - taṉatu talaimaiyai aṟintu taṉ kaṭamaikaḷaic celuttavum, maṇaṉ iṭaiyākak koḷḷāṉ - maṇattiṉ vāyilākak koḷḷāmalum,
4-8. kal paka kaṇa maḻai tuṟanta - pāṟaikaḷ piḷavupaṭak kūṭṭamākiya mēkam peyyā toḻinta, ēntu koṭi paranta kāṉ mayaṅku aḻuvam - uyarnta koṭi paranta kāṭaṭarnta parappukkaḷ, poṟi vari alkul māyōṭku - puḷḷikaḷum varikaḷum poruntiya alkulaiyuṭaiya māmai niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyāṭku, eḷiyavāka eṉa - naṭattaṟku eḷimaiyuṭaiyaṉavāka veṉṟu, taṇinta paruvam cellāṉ - veppam taṇinta paruvattē cellāmalum,
10-16. kaṭum pakaṭṭu oruttal - kaṭiya perumaiyaiyuṭaiya āṇ yāṉai, naṭuṅkak kutti - maram acaiyumpaṭi kutti, pōḻ puṇ paṭutta - piḷattalāl puṇ uṇṭākkiya, pori arai ōmai - porikaḷ poruntiya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ōmai marattiṉ, perum poḷi cēy arai nōkki - periya paṭṭai yurinta civanta aṭi marattiṉai nōkki, ūṉ cettu - ataṉai ūṉ eṉak karuti, cēṇ uyarntu ōṅkiya - neṭitu uyarnta, karuṅ kāl yāttu - kariya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya yā marattiluḷḷa, paruntu vantu iṟukkum - paruntu vantu taṅkum, vāṉ uyar neṭuṅkōṭṭu - vāṉiṉkaṇ uyarnta neṭiya cimaiyaṅkaḷiluḷḷa, kōṭai vevvaḷikku ulamarum - vevviya mēl kāṟṟiṉāl alaiyum, pul ilai vetira nel viḷai kāṭu - pulliya ilaikaḷaiyuṭaiya mūṅkiliṉ nel viḷaiyum kāṭṭiṉkaṇ,
8-9. paṭartarat tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa - uṭaṉkoṇṭu cella oru talaiyākat tuṇintāṉ.
(muṭipu) tuṉai veṅ kāḷai, yāṉ pārāṭṭa, tāy kaṭaṉ iṟuppa viyaṉakar maṇaṉ iṭaiyākak koḷḷāṉ, kāṉmayaṅku aḻuvam māyōṭku eḷiyavāka eṉat taṇinta paruvam cellāṉ, nel viḷai kāṭu paṭartarat tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa.
(vi-rai) taṉ cemmal - talaivaṉatu māṇpu. muḻavu mukam pularāmaiyāvatu - iṭaiyaṟātu olittal. maṇaṉ iṭaiyākak koḷḷāṉ - varaintu koḷḷāṉāy eṉṟapaṭi. ēntu koṭi paranta eṉṟataṉai alkulukku aṭaiyākki, uyarnta koṭipōṟ paranta varikaḷaiyuṭaiya eṉṟalumām. māyōṭku eḷiyavāka eṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka. tuṉaital - viraital, poṟumaiyillāmai eṉṟapaṭi. yāṉai uṇṭaṟ poruṭṭuk kuttip puṇpaṭutta ōmaiyiṉ arai eṉka. yāṉai ōmaiyai urittut tiṉṉumeṉpatu `kayantalai maṭappiṭi yuyaṅku paci kaḷaiiyar; peruṅkaḷiṟu tolaitta muṭattā ḷōmai'1 `kāṉa yāṉai tōṉayan tuṇṭa poritā ḷōmai'2 eṉpaṉavaṟṟālum aṟika. ōṅkiya yāttu eṉka. iṟukkum kāṭu eṉavum, neṭuṅkōṭṭu vetira kāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
398. kuṟiñci
(kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāl (varaiviṭattukkaṇ) talaimakaḷ talaimakaṉ varaiyiṉiṉṟum pōnta āṟṟoṭu pulantu colliyatu.)
		3iḻainilai nekiḻnta evvaṅ kūrap
		paṭarmali varuttamoṭu palapulan tacaii
		meṉṟōḷ nekiḻac cāayk koṉṟai
		ūḻuṟu malariṟ pāḻpaṭa muṟṟiya
	5.	pacalai mēṉi nōkki nutalpacantu
		iṉṉē mākiya emmivaṇ aruḷāṉ
		nummōṉ ceyta koṭumaikku immeṉṟu
		alamaral maḻaikkaṇ teṇpaṉi malka
		naṉṟu 4puṟa māṟiakaṟal yāḻaniṉ
	10.	kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṟ keṉṉeṉap paṭumō
		karaiporu nīttam uraiyeṉak kaḻaṟi
		niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci avarmalaip
		paṉmalar pōrttu nāṇumika oṭuṅki
		maṟaintaṉai kaḻiyum niṟṟantu celutti
	15.	nayaṉaṟat tuṟattal valli yōrē
		notuma lāḷar atuka ṇōṭātu
		aḻaṟciṉai vēṅkai niḻaṟṟavirn tacaii
		māri puṟantara nanti āriyar
		poṉpaṭu neṭuvarai puraiyum entai
	20.	palpūṅ kāṉat talki iṉṟivaṇ
		cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuva tuṇṭō
		kuyavari irumpōttup poruta puṇkūrntu
		uyaṅkupiṭi taḻīiya mataṉaḻi yāṉai
		vāṅkamaik kaḻaiyiṉ naralumavar
	25.	ōṅkumalai nāṭṭiṉ varūu vōyē.
- immeṉkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. karai poru nīttam - karaiyaip porutu varum veḷḷam poruntiya yāṟē!
22-25. kuyavari irupōttu poruta puṇ kūrntu - periya āṇ puli tākkiyatālākiya puṇ mikuntu, uyaṅku piṭi taḻīiya mataṉ aḻi yāṉai - varuntum peṇ yāṉaiyāl taḻuvappaṭum vali kuṉṟiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai, vāṅku amai kaḻaiyiṉ naralum - vaḷainta mūṅkilāṟ ceyta tūmpu pōla olikkum, avar ōṅku malai nāṭṭiṉ varūuvōy - em talaivaratu uyarnta malai nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum varuvōy!
1-16. iḻai nilai nekiḻnta evvam kūra - aṇikaḷ nilaiyiṉiṉṟum nekiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya tuṉpam mika, paṭar mali varuttamoṭu - niṉaivu mikka varuttattuṭaṉ, pala pulantu acaii - palavaṟṟaiyum veṟuttuk kūṟittaṅki, mel tōḷ nekiḻa - melliya tōḷ meliya, cāay - varunti, koṉṟai ūḻ uṟu malaril - koṉṟaiyiṉatu naṉku malarnta pūkkaḷaippōla, pāḻpaṭa muṟṟiya pacalai mēṉi nōkki - pāḻpaṭa niṟainta pacalaiyuṟṟa mēṉiyaip pārttu, nutal pacantu - neṟṟi pacalaiyuṟappeṟṟu, iṉṉēm ākiya em ivaṇ aruḷāṉ - iṉṉa nilaiyiṉēm ākiya emmai iṅku aruḷutal ceyyāṉāy, nummōṉ ceyta koṭumaikku - num talaivaṉ ceyta vaṉkaṇmaikku, immeṉṟu alamaral maḻai kaṇ teṇ paṉi malka - kalaṅkum  kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum viraintu teḷinta nīr perukavum, naṉṟu puṟam māṟi akaṟal - aṟattiṉaik kaiviṭṭu nīṅkutal, niṉ kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku eṉ eṉappaṭumō - kuṉṟu poruntiya nāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya niṉ talaivaṉukku yāteṉappaṭumō, urai eṉak kaḻaṟi - uraip
pāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci - niṉṉoṭu yām veṟuttalai añci, avar malai pala malar pōrttu - avar malaiyiluḷḷa pala malarkaḷaiyum pōrttuk koṇṭu, nāṇu mika oṭuṅki - nāṇattāl mikavum oṭuṅki, maṟaintaṉai kaḻiyum - maṟaintu nīṅkum, niṉ tantu celutti - niṉṉaik koṇarntu pōkki, notumalāḷar - ētilāḷarākiya avar, nayaṉ aṟat tuṟattal valliyōr - nītiyiṉṟik kaiviṭutaṟku vaṉmaiyuṭaiyōr āvar, atu kaṇ ōṭātu - ataṉai nōkkātu,
17-21. aḻal ciṉai vēṅkai niḻal tavirntu acaii - tīyiṉai yokkum pūkkaḷ niṟainta kiḷaiyaiyuṭaiya vēṅkaiyiṉ niḻalil niṉ celaviṉait tavirntu taṅki, māri puṟantara nanti - peyyum maḻai purattalāl perukkamuṟṟu, āriyar poṉpaṭu neṭu varai puraiyum - āriyaratu poṉ poruntiya nīṇṭa imaiya malaiyai yokkum, entai palpūṅ kāṉattu alki - em tantaiyatu pala pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭil amarntu, iṉṟu ivaṇ cērntaṉai celiṉē - iṟṟaip
poḻutu ivviṭattē cērntiruntu celliṉ, citaikuvatu uṇṭō - niṉ kāriyam ētēṉum keṭuvatu uṇṭō.
(muṭipu) karai poru nīttam! avar ōṅku malai nāṭṭiṉ varūuvōy! evvam kūra, varuttamoṭu acaii tōḷ nekiḻac cāay, pacalai mēṉi nōkki nutal pacantu iṉṉēmākiya em ivaṇ aruḷāṉ nummōṉ ceyta koṭumaikku, kaṇ paṉi malka, naṉṟu puṟamāṟi akaṟal, niṉ kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku eṉṉeṉap paṭumō urai eṉak kaḻaṟip pulattal añci, malarpōrttu oṭuṅki maṟaintaṉai kaḻiyum niṟṟantu celutti notumalāḷar tuṟattal valliyōr, atu kaṇ ōṭātu vēṅkai niḻal tavirntu acaii, nanti, kāṉattu alki, ivaṇ cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuvatu uṇṭō.
(vi-rai) nekiḻnta - nekiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya. pāḻpaṭa - vaṟitē. nummōṉ - numaṉ, num talaivaṉ. naṉṟu - aṟam. puṟam māṟal - kai viṭutal. kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku - cuṭṭu. eṉ eṉap paṭumō - yātākumō eṉṟapaṭi. nummōṉ eṉavum niṉ kuṉṟu keḻunāṭaṉ eṉavum kūṟiṉamaiyāl, kaḻaṟutalum pulattalum yāṟṟiṉ mēla āyiṉa. maṟaintu kaḻitalāvatu malar pōrttuk kaḻital. niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci nī malar pōrttu oṭuṅki maṟaintu kaḻiyāniṉṟaṉai, aṅṅaṉamākavum notumalāḷar niṉṉait tantu celuttit tuṟattal valliyōr āvar eṉa virittu uraittuk koḷka. kaṇ ōṭātu - nōkkātu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. nantutal - perukutal. iṉṟu ivaṇ cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuvatu uṇṭō eṉṟatu, ataṉāl niṉakku varakkaṭavatoru tīṅku illāmaiyoṭu avaratu malaināṭṭiṉiṉṟum varukiṉṟā yākaliṉ niṉṉuṭaṉ oru nāḷ taṅki yāmum āṟṟiyiruttalumākum eṉṟu kaḻipaṭar tōṉṟak kūṟiyavāṟu. kuyavari - kuyampōlum varikaḷaiyuṭaiya puli. kuyam - arivāḷ. vali yaḻinta yāṉai tūmpupōl uyirkkum eṉpatu. `ōy kaḷi ṟeṭutta nōyuṭai neṭuṅkai, toku coṟ kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum'1 eṉa muṟ pōntataṉālum aṟika. kaḻai eṉṟatu īṇṭut tūmpu eṉṉum poruṭṭu.
399. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		cimaiyak kurala cāntarunti iruḷi
		imaiyak kāṉam nāṟuṅ kūntal
		naṉṉutal arivai iṉṉuṟal ākam
		paruku vaṉṉa kātal uḷḷamoṭu
	5.	tirukupu muyaṅkal iṉṟiyavaṇ nīṭār
		kaṭaṟṟaṭai maruṅkiṉ kaṇicciyiṟ kuḻitta
		uṭaikkaṇ nīṭamai ūṟal uṇṭa
		pāṭiṉ teṇmaṇip payaṅkeḻu perunirai
		vāṭupulam pukkeṉak kōṭutuvait takaṟṟi
	10.	olkunilaik kaṭukkai alkuniḻal acaiip
		pallāṉ kōvalar kallātu ūtum
		ciṟuvetirn tīṅkuḻaṟ pulampukoḷ teḷviḷi
		maiyil paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tōṟṟap
		palkōḷ nellip paiṅkāy arunti
	15.	melkiṭu maṭamarai ōrkkum attam
		kāykatir kaṭukiya kaviṉaḻi piṟaṅkal
		vēykaṇ uṭainta cimaiya
		vāypaṭu maruṅkiṉ malaiyiṟan tōrē.
- eyiṉantai makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. cimaiya kurala cāntu arunti iruḷi - ucciyiluḷḷa pūṅkottukkaḷai yuṭaiyaṉavāy mayirccāntiṉaik koṇṭu iruṇṭu, imaiya kāṉam nāṟum - imaiya malaiyiluḷḷa kāṉam pōla nāṟum, kūntal - kūntalaiyum, nalnutal - nalla neṟṟiyiṉaiyu
muṭaiya, arivai - talaiviyē!
6-18. kaṭaṟu aṭai maruṅkiṉ - kaṟ kāṭṭiṉaic cārnta pakkattil, kaṇicciyil kuḻitta - kuntāliyāl tōṇṭiṉa, uṭaikkaṇ - kuḻiyiṭattē, nīṭu amai ūṟal uṇṭa - tāḻttal poruntiya ūṟṟu nīrai yuṇṭa, pāṭu iṉ teḷ maṇi - iṉiya teḷinta ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya maṇikaḷaip pūṇṭa, payam keḻu peru nirai - payaṉ mikka periya āṉirai, vāṭu pulam pukkeṉa - vaṟaṇṭa pālaiyiṟ pukuntatāka, kōṭu tuvaittu akaṟṟi - ūtu kompiṉāl oliyai yuṇṭākki avaṟṟai aṅku niṉṟum vilakki, olku nilai kaṭukkai alku niḻal acaii - taḷarnta nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya koṉṟaiyiṉ kuṟainta niḻalil taṅkuvittu, pal āṉ kōvalar - pala āṉiṉaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya āyar, kallātu ūtum - icaiyaik kallāmal ūtum, ciṟu vetir tīm kuḻal - ciṟiya mūṅkilāṟ ceyta iṉiya kuḻaliṉiṉṟeḻum, pulampu koḷ teḷ viḷi - taṉimai koṇṭa teḷinta ōcaiyiṉai, mai il paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tōṟṟap palkōḷ nellip paiṅkāy arunti - kuṟṟamaṟṟa paḷiṅkiṉaip pōṉṟa tōṟṟattaiyuṭaiya palavākak kāytta nelliyiṉ paciya kāykaḷai uṇṭu, melkiṭu maṭa marai - acaiyiṭukiṉṟa iḷaiya maraimāṉ, ōrkkum - kūrntu kēṭkum, attam - kāṭṭilē, kāy katir kaṭukiya kaviṉ aḻi piṟaṅkal - kāyum ñāyiṟu muṭukiya aḻaku oḻinta pāṟaikaḷaiyum, vēy kaṇ uṭainta cimaiyam - mūṅkil kaṇukkaḷ uṭainta cikaraṅ kaḷaiyum, vāy paṭu maruṅkiṉ - iṭamakaṉṟa pakka malaiyiṉaiyu muṭaiya, malai iṟantōr - malaiyiṉaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talaivar,
3-5. iṉ uṟal ākam - niṉatu iṉimai poruntutalai yuṭaiya mārpiṉai, paruku aṉṉa kātal uḷḷamoṭu - parukutal pōlum kātal mikka uḷḷattuṭaṉ, tirukupu muyaṅkal iṉṟi- piṉṉit taḻuvutal iṉṟi, avaṇ nīṭār - aṅkut tāḻttirār.
(muṭipu) naṉṉutal arivai! malaiyiṟantōr, niṉ iṉ uṟal ākam kātal uḷḷamoṭu muyaṅkal iṉṟi, avaṇ nīṭār.
(vi-rai) cimaiyam - maram putarkaḷiṉ ucci. kural - pūṅkottu. kuralvāy nāṟuṅ kūntal eṉka. tirukutal - oruvar meyyuṭaṉ oruvar mey piṉṉutal. uṭai - uṭaitteṭutta kiṇaṟṟiṉaik kuṟippa tāyiṟṟu. payam - payaṉ, pāl. kallātu ūtum - āciriyaṉiṭattuk kaṟkāmalē iyalpāka ūtum eṉka. kōvalar ūtum tīṅkuḻaliṉ teḷviḷiyai marai ōrkkum attattilē piṟaṅkalum cimaiyamum maruṅkum poruntiya malai yiṟantu ceṉṟōr eṉka.
400. neytal
(talaimakaṉ varainteytiya piṉṟait tōḻi talaimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		nakainaṉ ṟamma tāṉē avaṉoṭu
		maṉaiyiṟan talkiṉum alareṉa nayantu
		kāṉal alkiya naṅkaḷa vakalap
		palpurin tiyaṟal uṟṟa nalviṉai
	5.	1nūlamai piṟappiṉ nīla uttik
		koymayir eruttam piṇarpaṭap peruki
		neymmiti muṉaiiya koḻuñcōṟ ṟārkai
		niraliyain toṉṟiya celaviṟ centiṉaik
		kuralvārnta taṉṉa 2kulavuttalai naṉṉāṉku
	10.	vīṅkucuval mociyat tāṅku 3nukam taḻīip
		pūmpoṟip palpaṭai olippap pūṭṭi
		matiyuṭai valavaṉ ēvaliṉ ikutuṟaip
		puṉalpāyn taṉṉa vāmāṉ tiṇṭērk
		kaṇaikaḻin taṉṉa nōṉkāl vaṇpari
	15.	pālkaṇ ṭaṉṉa ūtai veṇmaṇal
		kālkaṇ ṭaṉṉa vaḻipaṭap pōki
		ayirccēṟ ṟaḷḷal aḻuvat tāṅkaṇ
		iruṇīr iṭṭuccuram nīntit tuṟaikeḻu
		mellam pulampaṉ vanta ñāṉṟaip
	20.	pūmali iruṅkaḻit tuyalvarum aṭaiyoṭu
		nēmi tanta neṭunīr neytal
		viḷaiyā iḷaṅkaḷ nāṟap palavuṭaṉ
		1potiyaviḻ taṇmalar kaṇṭu naṉṟum
		putuva tākiṉ ṟamma paḻaviṟaṟ
	25.	pāṭeḻun tiraṅkum munnīr
		nīṭirum peṇṇai nam aḻuṅkal ūrē.
- ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. (tōḻi,) maṉai iṟantu alkiṉum - maṉaiyaik kaṭantu taṅkiṉum, alar eṉa - alarākum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭavum, avaṉoṭu nayantu kāṉal alkiya nam kaḷavu akala - nam talaivaṉuṭaṉ virumpic cōlaiyilē ceṉṟu taṅkiya nam kaḷavoḻukkam nīṅkumpaṭi, pal purintu iyaṟal uṟṟa nalviṉai - muṟ piṟappil pala naṉmaikaḷum ceytu poruntutaluṟṟa nalviṉaiyāl,
5-19. nūl amai piṟappiṉ - puravi nūl kūṟum ilakkaṇam amainta piṟappiṉaiyum, nīla utti - nīla maṇiyālākiya neṟṟic cuṭṭiyiṉaiyum, koy mayir eruttam piṇarpaṭap peruki - caruccarai yuṇṭāka aṭarntu koyyappeṟṟa mayir poruntiya piṭariyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, ney miti muṉaiiya koḻuñ cōṟṟu ārkai - ney peytu mitittiyaṟṟiya kavaḷattai veṟutta koḻuviya cōṟṟai uṇṇutalaiyuṭaiya, niral iyaintu oṉṟiya celavil - varicaiyākap porunti oru peṟṟiyē cellum celaviṉaiyum, centiṉai kural vārntaṉṉa kulavu talai - cevviya tiṉaiyiṉ katir nīṇṭu vaḷaintāṟ pōṉṟa vaḷainta talaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, nal nāṉku - naṉṟākiya nāṉku kutiraikaḷai, tāṅku nukam taḻīi - tāṅkum nukattiṉaip porunti, vīṅku cuval mociya - parutta kaḻuttil neruṅka (aṇinta), pūm poṟi pal paṭai olippa pūṭṭi,  aḻakiya puḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya pala vaṭaṅkaḷ olikkumpaṭi pūṭṭi, matiyuṭai valavaṉ ēvaliṉ - aṟivuṭaiya pākaṉ celuttaliṉ, iku tuṟai puṉal pāyntaṉṉa - tāḻnta tuṟaiyil nīr pāyntālotta, vām māṉtiṇ tēr - tāvic cellum kutirai pūṭṭiya tiṇṇiya tēriṉ, kaṇai kaḻintaṉṉa nōṉ kāl vaṇ pari - ampu pāyntāṟ pōṉṟa valiya kāliṉ virainta celavu, pāl kaṇṭaṉṉa ūtai veṇmaṇal - pālaik kaṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa kāṟṟāl tiraṭṭappeṟṟa veḷḷiya maṇaliṭattē, kāl kaṇṭaṉṉa vaḻipaṭa pōki - vāykkālaik kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa cuvaṭu uṇṭākap pōka, ayir cēṟṟu aḷḷal aḻuvattu āṅkaṇ - nuṇṇiya kuḻampākiya cēṟṟiṉ parappiṉaiyuṭaiya avviṭattē, iruḷ nīr iṭṭu curam nīnti - iruṇṭa nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟukiya kaḻiyaik kaṭantu, tuṟai keḻu mel am pulampaṉ - tuṟai - poruntiya kaṭaṟ karaiyaiyuṭaiya nam talaivaṉ, vanta ñāṉṟai - vanta aṉṟu,
20-23. pūmali iru kaḻi tuyalvarum aṭaiyoṭu - pūkkaḷ niṟainta kariya kaḻiyiṭattē ilaikaḷōṭu acaiyum, nēmi tanta neṭu nīr neytal - āḻamāṉa nīraiyuṭaiya kaṭal tanta neytaliṉ, viḷaiyā iḷaṅ kaḷ nāṟa - mutirāta iḷaiya kaḷ nāṟumpaṭi, poti aviḻ taṇ malar - kaṭṭu aviḻnta taṇṇiya malarkaḷai, palavuṭaṉ kaṇṭu - ēṉaip pūkkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ (cūṭi varutalaik) kaṇṭu,
23-26. paḻa viṟal pāṭu eḻuntu iraṅkum - paḻaiya perumaiyuṭaiya āravāram mikku muḻaṅkum, munnīr - kaṭaṟkaraik kaṇṇuḷḷa, nīṭu iru peṇṇai - nīṇṭa periya paṉaimarattai yuṭaiya, nam aḻuṅkal ūr - alar āravāram mikka namatu ūr, naṉṟum putuvatu ākiṉṟu- (anta alariṉai viṭuttu) mikavum putiya taṉmaiyiṉai uṭaiyatu ākiṉṟatu,
1. nakai naṉṟu - atu, peritum nakaiyiṉai viḷaippatākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṉal alkiya nam kaḷavu akala, nalviṉaiyāl, mellam pulampaṉ iruḷnīr iṭṭuc curam nīnti vanta ñāṉṟai, avaṉ malar cūṭi varutalaik kaṇṭu, nam aḻuṅ
kalūr naṉṟum putuvatākiṉṟu, amma! atu nakai naṉṟu, amma!
(vi-rai) kāṉal alkiya - maṉaiyiṉ aḷavilaṉṟik kāṭṭilum ceṉṟu taṅkiya eṉka. kaḷavu akala vanta ñāṉṟai eṉavum, nal viṉaiyāl vanta ñāṉṟai eṉavum kūṭṭuka. neymmiti - koḷḷuṭaṉ ney peytu amaitta kavaḷam. nāṉku - nāṉkākiya kutiraikaḷ, ākupeyar. piṟappiṉaiyum uttiyiṉaiyum eruttattiṉaiyum uṭaiya nāṉku eṉavum, ārkaiyuṭaiya nāṉku eṉavum, celaviṉaiyum talaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya nāṉku eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. mociya aṇinta palpaṭai eṉṟu viritturaikka. paṭai eṉṟatu kaḻuttu aṇikaḷai. pōki - pōka eṉat tirikka. tiṇtēr nōṉ kāl veṇmaṇal vaḻipaṭap pōka eṉka. vanta ñāṉṟai - vantu varainta ñāṉṟai. aṭaiyoṭu tuyalvarum eṉavum neṭunīr nēmi eṉavum māṟik kūṭṭuka. neytaliṉ taṇ malaraip palavuṭaṉ cūṭi varutal kaṇṭu eṉka. nāṟa eṉṉum viṉaiyeccam aviḻ eṉṉum viṉaikoṇṭu muṭintatu. talaivaṉ varain teytutal kaṇṭu, ūr alar kūṟutal aṭaṅkiviṭṭa teṉpāḷ, aḻuṅkal ūr putuvatākiṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. amma iraṇṭum uraiyacai iṭaiccol.
eṭṭut tokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya neṭuntokai 
yeṉappaṭum akanāṉūṟṟukku nāvalar paṇṭita, na.mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭārum, karantaik kaviyaracu, ār. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷaiyum iyaṟṟiya "vēṅkaṭa viḷakku" eṉṉum urai muṟṟiṟṟu.
*****


pāyiram
		niṉṟa nīti veṉṟa nēmip
		paḻutil koḷkai vaḻutiya ravaikkaṇ
		aṟivuvīṟ ṟirunta ceṟivuṭai maṉattu
		vāṉṟōy nallicaic cāṉṟōr kuḻīi
	5.	aruntamiḻ mūṉṟun terinta kālai
		āynta koḷkait tīntamiḻp pāṭṭuḷ
		neṭiya vāki yaṭinimirn toḻukiya
		iṉpap pakuti yiṉporuṭ pāṭal
		nāṉū ṟeṭuttu nūṉavil pulavar
	10.	kaḷitta mummatak kaḷiṟṟi yāṉainirai
		maṇiyoṭu miṭainta aṇikiḷar pavaḷam
		mēviya nittilak kōvai yeṉṟāṅku
		attaku paṇpiṉ muttiṟa māka
		muṉṉiṉar tokutta naṉṉeṭun tokaikkuk
	15.	karutteṉap paṇpiṉō ruraittavai nāṭiṉ
		avvakaik kavaitām cevviya vaṉṟi
		ariyavai yākiya poruṇmai nōkkik
		kōṭṭa miṉṟip pāṭṭoṭu poruntat
		takavoṭu ciṟanta akaval naṭaiyāṟ
	20.	karuttiṉi tiyaṟṟi yōṉē parittēr
		vaḷavar kākkum vaḷanāṭ ṭuḷḷum
		nāṭeṉac ciṟanta pīṭukeḻu ciṟappiṟ
		keṭalaruñ ciṟappiṉ iṭaiyaḷa nāṭṭut
		tītil koḷkai mūtū ruḷḷum
	25.	ūreṉac ciṟanta cīrkeḻu maṇakkuṭic
		cemmai cāṉṟa tēvaṉ
		toṉmai cāṉṟa naṉmai yōṉē.
"it tokaikkuk karuttu akavalāṟ pāṭiṉāṉ, iṭaiyaḷa nāṭṭu maṇakkuṭiyāṉ pālvaṇṇa tēvaṉāṉa villava taraiyaṉ. neṭuntokai nāṉūṟum karuttiṉoṭu muṭintaṉa. ivai pāṭiṉa kavikaḷ nūṟṟu nāṟpattaivar.
ittokaip pāṭṭiṟ kaṭiyaḷavu ciṟumai patiṉmūṉṟu; perumai muppattoṉṟu. tokuppittāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti. tokuttāṉ maturai uppūri kuṭikiḻār makaṉāvāṉ uruttiracaṉmaṉ eṉpāṉ.
`vaṇṭupaṭat tatainta kaṇṇi' eṉpatu mutalāka `neṭuvēṇ mārpiṉ' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟṟirupatum kaḷiṟṟi yāṉai nirai yeṉappaṭum. ip peyar kāraṇattāṟ peṟṟatu; itu poruṭ kāraṇamākak koḷka. `nānakaiyuṭaiya neñcē; eṉpatu mutalāka `nāḷvalai mukanta' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟṟeṇpatum maṇimiṭai pavaḷam eṉappaṭum. ituvuṅ kāraṇap peyar; eṉṉai, ceyyuḷum poruḷum ovvāmaiyāl. `vaṟaṉuṟu ceyti' eṉpatu mutalāka `nakainaṉṟamma' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟum nittilak kōvai yeṉappaṭum; ceyyuḷum poruḷum okkumākalāṉ.'
akanāṉūṟṟiṉ tokuppu muṟai
		viyamellām veṇṭē riyakkam; kayamalarnta
		tāmaraiyā ṟākat takaipeṟīik kāmar
		naṟumullai nāṉkāka nāṭṭi veṟimāṇṭa
		eṭṭum iraṇṭum kuṟiñciyāk kuṭṭattu
		ivartirai pattā iyaṟpaṭa yāttāṉ
		tokaiyi ṉeṭiyataṉait tōlāc ceviyāṉ
		vakaiyi ṉeṭiyataṉai vaippu.
		oṉṟu mūṉṟain tēḻoṉ pāṉpālai; ōtātu
		niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ nāṉku neṟimullai; - aṉṟiyē
		āṟām marutam; aṇineytal aiyiraṇṭu;
		kūṟāta vaikuṟiñcik kūṟṟu.
		pālai viyamellām; pattām paṉineytal;
		nālu naḷimullai nāṭuṅkāl; - mēlaiyōr
		tēṟum iraṇṭeṭ ṭivaikuṟiñci; centamiḻiṉ
		āṟu marutam akam.
viyam - oṟṟaiyeṇkaḷ. veṇṭē riyakkam - pālai. tāmarai- marutam. kuṭṭattu ivar tirai - neytal. tōlāc ceviyāṉ - kēḷviyuṇarvu mikkavaṉ.
akanāṉūṟu - nittilak kōvai
